Recreate the brain's knowledge FOUNDATION + glass-box REASONING, NO external LLM at runtime. TWO MAIN TASKS: (1) READING (glass-box, no-LLM) + (2) FOUNDATION (grow it). MEMORY = complementary machinery. Brain = north-star + existence-proof (a "wall" = an implementation limit, never impossible).

**THE PICTURE (this session's synthesis):** the parser, the discourse "state of mind," and the durable foundation are ONE architecture -- a GROWN form-meaning construction inventory + a nested working-memory overlay + the surprisal primitive, running GROW -> USE -> CONSOLIDATE with top-down bidirectional feedback.
- **5x BARRIER SWEEP (what blocks glass-box real-prose reading):** RANKED #1 construction-BREADTH > #2 lexicon/selectional > #3 prediction > #4 working-memory > #5 foundation-size. USER hypothesis ("just foundation/WM") REFUTED. WM's useful part (incremental structural memory) FOLDS INTO the parser; distinct WM lever = interference-aware retrieval for coref.
- **HOW THE BRAIN PARSES BROADLY:** a large LEARNED construction inventory (form-meaning, all grain sizes, no lexicon/grammar divide), GROWN usage-based (ENTRENCHMENT/frequency), USED predictively (surprisal) with top-down feedback. => brain-faithful #1 build = GROW constructions from reading, NOT bolt-on a fixed PCFG (plain PCFG = "installed", syntax-only). Engineering convergence: DOP/grammar-induction = closest analog.
- **PRIMITIVE UNIFICATION:** SURPRISAL (-log P) does the USE jobs (ingest-gate + parse-retrieval + discourse event-segmentation); ENTRENCHMENT (frequency) does ACQUISITION (grow the inventory). Two primitives, two roles (VET-corrected: surprisal is NOT the acquisition selector).

ACTIVE WM = ~4-chunk FOCUS (resonator, FACTORIZATION-bound ~3-7, does NOT scale with N -- brain-parity) nested in an N/16 ACTIVATED POOL (scales LINEARLY with N: 64@N=1024, 2000@N=32000). PER-BUFFER ~N/7 dense (146@1024, ~4500@32000) or ~256xblock block-sparse (~16k). COMPUTE ~1000x/query cheap. DECOUPLED per subsystem (VET-confirmed). KEY INSIGHT: N controls STORAGE not THINKING-WIDTH -- crank N for the store/pool (linear gain), keep the focus small (factorization-bound), escape the ~4-focus via CHUNKING (depth) + PAGING (breadth), like the brain. => brain-parity architecture + lossless-storage edge; constraint = active-window (reason-over-simultaneity ~4).**
**🧠 FOCUS-LIMIT -- DRILL LANDED (a4b1b9c2, note research_wm_focus_limit_functional_increase_2026-07-17.md, P=0.42; USER-asked intuitive). CONFIRMED: the ~4 limit is a CONFUSION (interference/mis-binding: Oberauer swap-errors, worse with SIMILAR items) problem, NOT a SPACE problem -- true in BOTH brain (theta-gamma phase coding: theta=order, gamma=maintain, alpha=suppress-clutter) AND resonator. LITERATURE-CONFIRMED (Frady et al. 2020 Resonator Networks, not just our analogy): "operational capacity" scales with N; the F=3-4 joint-factor peak does NOT -- storage-capacity and joint-factor-capacity are GENUINELY SEPARATE axes (closes the 07-16 open flag). => "bigger N fixes focus" was never going to work; N = storage knob, focus = separate un-mixing knob pinned ~4. RAISE IT functionally (ranked, w/ downsides): (1) HIERARCHICAL/SEQUENTIAL FACTORIZATION [recommended #1, mostly BUILT] = resolve <=4 factors, bind result as ONE placeholder, recurse (chunking/Gobet-template + Hierarchical Resonator Nets); ~2-3 depth ceiling then noise compounds; needs SENSIBLE grouping (schema). (2) MULTIPLE DECOUPLED PARALLEL FOCI [NEW lever, P=0.30, Frontier-2 beat-the-brain] = N independent orthogonal channels WITHIN ONE MIND each running own ~F=3-4 that must eventually BIND together; brain built only 2 (verbal+spatial, Baddeley) by evolution, substrate NOT capped at 2; downside = only helps pre-separable content, cross-channel binding pays full cost, mis-routing = new failure mode. **USER-DISTINGUISHED (do not conflate): this WITHIN-mind hard version != the ACROSS-mind FREE version. MULTI-USER / MULTI-INSTANCE (each user gets own focus, foci NEVER touch = "just another interface") = EMBARRASSINGLY PARALLEL, LLM-batch model: shared read-only foundation (like frozen weights) + per-session WM overlay (like per-request KV-cache); ceiling = hardware throughput, NOT the ~4 wall (which only bites WITHIN a single focus). Only non-free case = concurrent WRITES to the shared foundation (users collaborating) need coordination; concurrent reads/independent reasoning need none.** (3) EXACT PAGING + (4) CACHED SWAP [Frontier-2, downside NEARLY ELIMINATED vs brain] = exact store has no reliability problem (beats notebook/memory-palace), exact cache has ~0 residual switch-cost (beats Monsell). (5) sparse/decorrelated coding = WRONG axis for focus (fixes STORAGE ~85x, NOT F -- Prediction 3, do not conflate). NOT-WORK: naive over-stuffing = precision-blur AND swap-errors both rise. HONEST: brain-faithful ~4 = real interference bound (accept + hierarchical-chunk/page); parallel-foci raise = open candidate (P=0.30). CHEAP DECISIVE CELL DEFINED (Prediction 1, candidate next build): F=6/8 flat vs 2-stage-hierarchical vs decorrelated-only on same codebooks -> HARD-PASS if hierarchical recovers >=90% of F=4 baseline while flat craters + decorr shows no F-peak shift.**
**🌳 RECURSIVE PAGED FOCUS-TREE = INTERNAL ANALOG OF AGENT ORCHESTRATION (USER 07-17 architectural direction, queued follow-up to the focus-lever cell): a master focus holds ~4 handles, each handle a compressed pointer that expands into its OWN ~4-way focus, recursively (4-ary tree matches the F=3-4 sweet spot; 4^depth reach = 4/16/64/256, only ~4 ever live at any active node). Each node ABSTRACTS its subtree into ONE clean handle for its parent = the SAME schematization/abstraction lever as the language-breadth build; abstraction is the compression that keeps each parent's 4 slots clean. TWO REGIMES (honest ceiling split): (A) SIMULTANEOUS [whole tree live in superposition] = CAPPED ~2-3 nested levels before cleanup-noise compounds (Yngve depth / Now-or-Never; the drill's depth ceiling). (B) SEQUENTIAL/PAGED [each node consolidated to the EXACT store, traversed one at a time] = effectively UNBOUNDED depth -- a call-stack backed by decay-free exact storage; bounded by paging cost + disk, NOT the ~4 wall = a genuine BEAT-THE-BRAIN regime (needs the memory paging machinery). HARD REQUIREMENT: branching must follow REAL content structure (a schema) -- arbitrary 4-way splits don't compress (expert-chunking evaporates on random boards). UNIFICATION (the deep part): this recursive focus-tree is STRUCTURALLY the multi-agent orchestration pattern -- master delegating to sub-managers each returning a COMPRESSED SUMMARY not full context = exactly a focus-node abstracting its subtree into one handle. Substrate-internal focus architecture == external agent architecture (bounded fan-out per node + abstraction-on-return). STATUS: focus-lever cell a71824a1 tests RUNG 1 (2-stage hierarchical recover >=90% at F=6/8 + where the simultaneous-depth wall lands); the recursive/paged-tree build is the informed FOLLOW-UP (design it once we know where the depth wall actually bites -- do NOT fire blind).**
